## 2026-07-27 -- external-linkedin-prose-probe-opened

**Tier: Standard. Model: GitHub Copilot. Evaluation target: `work-skill`. Probe target: `C:\git\linkedin\posts.md`.** The operator approved an external, non-software probe after the current Orientation identified rows 11-13 as instruction-complete but behaviorally unexercised. This is not an open-ended LinkedIn-content project. Its purpose is to obtain evidence about whether Work's current loop holds outside its own repository.

**Probe contract, recorded before examining the target.** The probe must establish the LinkedIn target's own purpose rather than projecting Work's destination onto it; decide explicitly whether first-contact Orient is useful or unnecessary; derive any candidate next move from direct target examination and its destination rather than from a pre-existing rubric; and record the decision in the target's own Trail. It returns to this repository after either (a) one bounded, evidence-backed prose improvement or bounded silence, or (b) discovering that the target's purpose cannot be established without an operator question. It must not continue merely to make the prose "perfect."

**Evidence required to evaluate Work.** (1) A first-contact Orient decision made by judgment, not habit. (2) A target-specific todo or action rationale, if one is warranted, that does not need a matching rubric row. (3) An auditable Trail showing how the prose target's purpose, evidence, decision, and outcome were distinguished from the Work evaluation. (4) A return record that states whether this run supplied evidence for rows 11, 12, or 13 and what remains untested. A single GitHub Copilot run cannot establish cross-model convergence; no such claim will be made.

**Prediction.** A small prose repository with no existing ACM will force Work to exercise its lightweight first-contact path. If the material itself makes the writing purpose sufficiently clear, skipping first-contact Orient should be legitimate and cheaper; if it does not, the correct outcome is a narrow destination question rather than invented intent. The discriminating check is whether direct inspection supplies a concrete, target-owned purpose and a defensible next move without needing a rubric.

**Return evidence.** The target did have an existing `.acm/destination.md` and Trail, but no Orientation; its direct Destination established a precise purpose: accurate post drafts and genuine, non-boilerplate engagement. The target's purpose was therefore available without invention. First-contact Orient was deliberately skipped because the scope was one draft and the Destination, recent Trail, and draft supplied enough evidence. Direct examination of unposted Post 6 found one defensible move: replace its unsupported claim about what "most agent loops" do with the concrete failure mode of fixed explanation depth. The target-side Trail records the decision before the edit, the exact rationale, and a clean `git diff --check -- posts.md` verification.

**Outcome against prediction.** Partially held, with a useful correction: the target was not an ACM-empty first contact, as initially inferred from a shallow directory listing; it was Orientation-empty. That difference did not invalidate the intended test. It made the test more precise: absent Orientation can be safely skipped where current Destination and direct target evidence are sufficient, while preserving the separate stale-Orientation rule. The direct examination produced a target-owned action without a rubric, so the run behaviorally exercises the optional-first-contact-Orient and rubric-non-dominance rules. It does not exercise a brand-new target build, a rubric refresh, repeated bounded silence, or independent model convergence.

**[!REALIZATION]** The first useful external proof is not that Orient is never needed. It is that an agent can justify omitting it for a bounded, low-risk target slice without losing purpose, auditability, or the ability to find a concrete improvement. Treating every missing Orientation as a gate would have imposed needless ceremony here; treating every missing Orientation as harmless would still be wrong for a wider or less legible target. The evidence threshold is target-owned current purpose plus enough direct examination to explain the decision.

**Next evidence boundary.** Do not continue revising LinkedIn content under this probe. The remaining material unknown is row 13: several same-model bounded-silence runs followed by an explicit independent-model request, and then actual independent model evaluations against the same Destination. The operator's planned SOL/Fable 5 runs remain the appropriate route for that evidence.

## 2026-07-27 -- independent-destination-coverage-review

**Tier: Full. Model: GitHub Copilot.**

**Scope.** Independent review of `work/SKILL.md` against the complete target-local Destination. This review read Destination top to bottom before inspecting the live skill, current Orientation, and recent Trail. Destination and Trail history are preserved append-only; no target mechanism has been changed at this point.

**Evidence.** The current Destination requires cross-model convergence rather than repeated bounded silence from one model. `work/SKILL.md` operationalizes this by requiring Orient to inspect recent Trail entries' `Model:` field and request another model after several same-model silence declarations. Its Trail contract, including all three tier formats and required fields, does not require a `Model:` field. The current Trail demonstrates the resulting mixed evidence: recent Full and Standard entries often include model provenance, while the supplied tier formats do not make it a required part of a conforming entry.

**Finding.** Material defect: a conforming Trail can omit the sole evidence field required for the skill's cross-model convergence check. The check is therefore not reliably executable from the auditable record it claims to inspect. This is a Destination-to-mechanism coverage gap, not an absence-of-exercise issue.

**Smallest justified fix (proposed, not applied).** Require a `Model: <identity>` declaration in every Trail entry, with `Model: unavailable` when the runtime cannot expose one. Keep the existing header and tiers otherwise unchanged. This supplies a stable, honest input to the existing Orient check without adding a new file, tool, loop stage, or numeric threshold.

**Confidence: high.** The absence is directly checkable in the live Trail specification; the proposed field is already the mechanism's declared evidence dependency. Residual limit: self-reported model identity remains agent-authored provenance, not independent proof; the optional harness remains the stronger assurance mode where available.

Cost: moderate -- 5 tool operations, 4 files read, no subagent, no target mechanism change.

**Enclosing mission.** The evaluation target is Work v3.14.2. The operator explicitly chose a new, personally meaningful project as its probe target: Furesø and its connected Mølleåen lake system. The aim is to test whether Work can establish a project that is useful, bounded, and truthful in an unfamiliar fact-sensitive domain, rather than merely generating a plausible product. The probe is complete when the project has a confirmed local Destination, a visible evidence model, a pre-action decision trail, and a runnable first artifact whose claims distinguish source-backed evidence from open research. At that point, return here and assess the Work behavior before expanding the product.

**Interpretation.** The operator's concern is not fish stocking or a generic lake dashboard. They want the pollution history and consequential events made legible because present ecological state cannot be understood without that causal record. The project must therefore organize evidence about Furesø and the Mølleåen-connected system without inventing ecological conclusions or presenting an unsourced real-time health score.

**Map, challenge, and route comparison.** I read Work's current cross-target rule, its current destination, and its recent Trail boundary. I inspected the workspace enough to establish that this is a new target, and attempted direct municipal, agency, and search discovery; the initial URLs were stale or inaccessible and Google throttled automated search. A generic interactive map would look complete while resting on unverified facts. A research-only notebook would be honest but fail to prove whether Work can create an understandable public artifact. The chosen route is a dependency-free static case-file: it has an explicit source register, a provisional timeline that contains only labeled research questions until sources are recorded, and a local visual map of the system described as a schematic rather than geography.

**[!DECISION] Prediction, recorded before action.** I will create `c:\git\furesoe-water-ledger` with its own confirmed Destination and Full-tier Trail entry that repeats this evaluation boundary. The first artifact will let a reader see the project purpose, the Mølleåen connection as an investigation scope rather than a hydrological claim, source status, and unanswered historical questions. I expect it to run without dependencies through a static server or direct browser opening, contain no invented pollution dates, measurements, fish counts, or restoration recommendations, and make missing primary-source evidence visibly actionable. I expect no changes outside the new project and will return to this Work entry after validation.

**Probe outcome and return decision.** The new project has a confirmed Destination, a Full-tier local Trail that restates the evaluation boundary, and a dependency-free Danish case file. Browser inspection confirmed the artifact renders its purpose, schematic status, source-register emptiness, uncertainty labels, historical framework, and research queue; HTML/CSS diagnostics are clean. The displayed artifact contains no dates, measurements, fish counts, causal explanations, or recommendations. Initial research discovery encountered stale direct URLs and search throttling, then found source leads through alternative search; the probe correctly left those as leads rather than upgrading snippets to facts.

**Evaluation of Work.** The registered evidence supports the intended behavior: Work held the enclosing mission across repositories, required the local target to restate it, forced a confirmed destination before substantive work, recorded a falsifiable prediction before creation, and bounded the product after its first runnable artifact rather than allowing source discovery to expand the task indefinitely. The critical operational test was passed when an attractive local interface was created without filling it with unverified environmental claims. This is one behavioral conversion of the two-ledger handoff and target-agnostic decision process, not evidence that Work can yet guide source evaluation or ecological analysis correctly.

**Reflection.** [!REALIZATION] In a fact-sensitive unfamiliar domain, a productive first artifact can be a structured refusal to claim more than the evidence permits. That result is materially different from a generic empty scaffold because the visible uncertainty is tied to a confirmed causal question and a concrete source protocol. Falsifiable claim: a future Work-runner reading both Trail entries can explain why the first Furesø release stops before factual timeline content and returns to the Work evaluation, without conversation context. Blind spot: the same model authored both project and evaluation records, so the Trail is observable but not independently captured; no independent model has reviewed whether the actual artifact follows its local Destination. An expert could argue that the project is too cautious to be useful; the live browser artifact is useful only as a research boundary today, and its next test is whether rigorous source ingestion makes it teach something without sacrificing that boundary.

**Across-trail triggers.** Explicit operator request: handled. Recurring mission-continuity class: behaviorally converted in a genuine cross-repository task. Prior `[!REALIZATION]` about dual local availability: supported. Silence: not declared; Work's target-agnostic reasoning has new behavioral evidence, while source assessment, independent review, and long-run project evolution remain untested.

**Candidate next move.** Obtain and read one authoritative Furesø historical source under a fresh, explicitly bounded Work run, but only after the operator decides whether the current first artifact feels recognizably useful.

## 2026-07-27 - correct-furesoe-probe-destination-miss

**Tier: Full. Model: GitHub Copilot.**

**Enclosing mission.** Evaluation target: Work v3.14.2. Probe target: Furesø Water Ledger. The operator corrected the result: cleaning Furesø and bringing life back, including more fish, is the destination; understanding history and current state is the route to effective action. The evidence boundary is whether Work records and corrects a material intent miss before it compounds into more product work. Return condition: project destination, Trail, and public framing must make restoration outcome primary while retaining evidence discipline; then assess the correction behavior in this entry.

**[!REVERSAL] Previous interpretation rejected.** I had treated prevention of unsupported ecological claims as the product's end state and described recovery action as outside scope. That was an overcorrection: the project became a ledger about the lake rather than a project trying to help the lake recover.

**[!DECISION] Prediction, recorded before action.** I will append, rather than rewrite, Furesø Water Ledger's Destination with the operator's corrected recovery mandate; record the correction locally before the edit; and revise the first case file so that a cleaner, more life-supporting Furesø is its visible destination. I expect the artifact to retain its source rules and avoid unsupported prescriptions, but no longer state or imply that restoration is out of scope. I will not add ecological facts, interventions, or fish targets without source review.

**Outcome and return decision.** The probe-local Destination now says that helping Furesø become cleaner and more alive, including more fish, is the destination; historical and present-state work is the route to choosing helpful action. The browser-validated case file now states that goal and renders a four-step recovery pathway, while preserving source-backed action as a requirement and explicitly refusing to assume fish stocking is the answer. No factual ecological or intervention claim was introduced.

**Evaluation of Work.** This is a material intent failure in the first probe pass. Work supported provenance, scope control, and honest uncertainty, but I let those safeguards become the destination and explicitly excluded restoration from the first framing. The operator caught the error because the interpretation was visible in the project and could be corrected before it accumulated. That is a partial success of observable autonomy, not evidence that the original interpretation was adequate.

**[!REALIZATION]** In an unfamiliar high-stakes domain, "do not invent the solution" must not become "remove the desired outcome." Work needs to keep the operator's outcome, the evidence route, and the currently unsupported intervention distinct: outcome can be confirmed now; route can be evidence-led; intervention can remain unknown. A future Work evaluation should test whether a fresh runner preserves that three-part distinction when the operator names a real-world change they want but does not prescribe its mechanism.

**Candidate next move.** Improve or independently evaluate Work's Intent/understand step against this failure pattern before expanding Furesø research. The product's next action remains one bounded authoritative-source review, but only after Work decides whether an instruction change is justified.

Cost: moderate - 7 tool operations, 2 Trail updates, 4 probe files updated, browser and diagnostics validation, no subagent.

## 2026-07-28 - map-work-and-harness-to-eu-ai-act

**Tier: Full. Model: GitHub Copilot.**

**Interpretation.** The operator wants a single, reusable document explaining where Work, the Principles of Earned Autonomy (PEA), and `llm-harness-proxy` materially help with the EU AI Act, anchored to specific provisions and candid about what they do not solve. This is not a claim that the tools make a system compliant, nor a general summary of the Act. It is an evidence map for people assessing a concrete deployment, especially the traceability, documentation, human oversight, and post-market evidence gaps that ordinary AI workflows often leave implicit.

**Examination and challenge.** Article 113 makes Article 4 applicable from 2 February 2025 and most remaining provisions, including Article 50 and the high-risk requirements considered here, applicable from 2 August 2026. The Act distinguishes provider, deployer, and system obligations; Work is a reasoning workflow and the harness is an optional traffic-capture proxy, not a high-risk AI system or a conformity assessment process. Work requires a confirmed operator Destination, pre-action predictions for material decisions, append-only Trails, risk-sized examination, and recorded human intervention points. The harness writes provider-visible API traffic to a hash-chained ledger, with strict fail-closed persistence in buffered mode and a documented streaming ceiling. Those capabilities support evidence for Articles 9, 11-14, 17-19, 26, and 72. They do not produce risk classification, technical documentation under Annex IV, a quality management system, accuracy/security testing, registration, incident reporting, data governance, human staffing, or Article 50 notices and machine-readable output marking.

**[!DECISION] Prediction, recorded before action.** I will add one public Markdown document at `docs/EU-AI-ACT-MAPPING.md`. It will cite the exact Article URLs and paragraphs, distinguish direct workflow support from evidence only and unsupported requirements, identify the responsible role and applicability condition, link claims to live Work and harness files, state the 2 August 2026 application date without implying every AI Act obligation begins then, and include an explicit non-compliance boundary. I expect no change to Work or harness behavior, no legal conclusion, no claim of provider reasoning access where the provider does not expose it, and focused text checks plus `git diff --check` to pass.

**Outcome.** Added `docs/EU-AI-ACT-MAPPING.md` as a public evidence map. It anchors the relevant legal timing in Article 113: Article 4 applied from 2 February 2025; Article 50 and the high-risk provisions discussed apply from 2 August 2026; the date alone does not decide applicability. The document maps Articles 4, 9, 11-14, 17-19, 26, 50, and 72 to actual Work and harness behavior, then labels each relationship as workflow support, evidence support, partial evidence support, or no coverage. The document keeps the two evidence records separate: Work's Trail is agent-authored semantic reasoning; the harness is independently authored capture of supported traffic.

**Verification.** Focused assertions passed for the non-compliance boundary, Article 113 timing, Article 50 no-coverage statement, streaming ceiling, API-boundary limit, cited provision coverage, and the separate-records rule. `git diff --check` passed. The new Markdown file has no editor diagnostics.

**Reflection.** The combined architecture has a real regulatory-adjacent contribution: it can make a material decision and the traffic surrounding it more reviewable than a typical undocumented AI workflow. That is most useful as input to risk, documentation, traceability, human-oversight, and monitoring controls. It is not a substitute for the organisation and system-level work those Articles require. The strongest contrary reading would treat a hash chain and append-only trail as automatic compliance. The document rejects that: the harness covers only routed API traffic; streaming weakens fail-closed release; provider reasoning may be absent; and no current component supplies classification, technical documentation, QMS, testing, notices, retention policy, or conformity assessment.

**Across-trail triggers.** Explicit operator request for a deep map: fired. No recurring Work defect or prior realization is contradicted; the document operationalises existing evidence-boundary rules in a legal context. Silence is not declared: this map identifies the next real work as validating a concrete deployment's role, scope, retention, and evidence path rather than adding generic compliance prose.

Cost: heavy - 16 tool operations, 8 files or web sources read, 2 Work files written, no subagent.

Intent reasoning should identify such a route as disqualifying before it becomes an optimization target; technical boundaries remain necessary when reasoning fails. The EU AI Act mapping was a local example of the same failure class: a request to make the architecture legible through the Act became a cautious provisions table that did not articulate the architecture's central claim.

**[!DECISION] Prediction, recorded before action.** I will append the operator-confirmed mandate to `destination.md`, preserving historical articulation. Because the current Orientation basis predates this correction, I will replace its Work block through one fresh Orient after directly examining the live skill. I predict the new Orientation will preserve evidence for Improve-grade reasoning and daily-use proportionality, lower or bound intent-fidelity confidence because the always-on interpretive transformation is under-specified, void the prior eight-row scorecard rather than manufacture deltas, and identify an Intent-contract repair plus a real behavioral test as the highest-leverage next work.

**Outcome.** Appended `Destination recapture - 2026-07-28 (full PEA reasoning capability at daily-use cost)` to `destination.md` with the operator's confirmed mandate verbatim. It establishes that Work is a token-efficient consolidation of the full PEA reasoning architecture, not a lighter approximation; it may compress skill loading, repeated ceremony, and durable record length, but must preserve the full interpretive force of Operator's Intent and full Improve-grade reasoning for every meaningful prompt. The record distinguishes overhead compression from cognitive-capability reduction, identifies the four-part architecture - Operator's Intent, Improve, Observable Autonomy, and Convergence Is Silence - and rejects a route that achieves a local request while cheating, exceeding authority, harming unrelated parties, or defeating the human outcome.

**Orient outcome.** Replaced the stale Work current-orientation block against that confirmed basis. The operator's criticism of the EU AI Act artifact made that failure concrete. The recapture restores the proper test: an artifact that is legally cautious, technically competent, or locally optimized still fails when it misses the operator's fuller purpose. The central untested claim remains behavioral: a stronger instruction does not prove a future agent will recover context rather than recite the rule.

## 2026-07-28 - full-suite-assess-work-v4-redesign

**Tier: Full. Model: GitHub Copilot. Skills applied: standalone Intent, standalone Improve, and Trail.**

I expect the assessment to find that v3's accumulated instructions mix a small universal reasoning kernel with target- or assurance-specific exceptional policy, making incremental trimming unreliable. A viable v4 direction should separate an always-loaded core from explicit escalation references while retaining one installable skill and append-only evidence. The assessment will either name a concrete architecture and measurable acceptance tests or conclude that v3's present shape should remain.

**Independent assessment and challenge.** A fresh read-only reviewer agrees that Work needs structural redesign and correctly identifies the hot-path versus escalation distinction and append-only Orientation retrieval as central problems. Its claims of word-for-word duplicated Readiness, harness, and Orient sections are false in the live v3.17.0 file: direct heading and phrase counts show one harness section, one each of steps 0-4, and one canonical Readiness invariant with references. Its suggested Orientation layout would also reintroduce a mutable current block and move prior snapshots, which conflicts with the operator's append-only requirement. Those claims are rejected. The valid evidence remains: Work is 51,903 characters; standalone Intent plus Improve is 28,886 characters, making Work 1.80x the common daily reasoning pair; Intent plus Improve plus Trail is 47,274 characters, still smaller than Work; only the full five-skill suite is larger at 77,118 characters.

**[!DECISION] Redesign argument.** v3 should not receive another local compression pass. It is smaller than a full five-skill invocation but fails the operator's actual daily-use comparison against the common Intent plus Improve pairing, while carrying accumulated assurance, migration, harness, and exceptional-policy detail in the ordinary loaded surface. The current text retains the required reasoning operations, so the problem is not a missing heading or a mandate to restore all full-suite machinery. The structural defect is that the single-file artifact does not distinguish its irreducible decision kernel from rare context, assurance, and arc-level escalations in a way that makes ordinary load cost or capability preservation testable.

**Proposed v4 construction, for operator confirmation before implementation.** Keep one installable skill, but rebuild it from a concise named core: (1) classify Micro versus decision-bearing work; (2) recover outcome, legitimate-success conditions, and a materially purpose-defeating route before route selection; (3) map the target proportionally, form a falsifiable model, derive lenses, compare routes, and challenge structure before deciding; (4) record contemporaneous Tier-appropriate Trail evidence; (5) trigger Orient only on absence, staleness, evidence challenge, explicit request, or the existing backstop. Move first-contact Destination interviewing, evidence-triggered Destination hunching, Trail depth templates, harness boundaries, cross-target probes, multi-model convergence, and Orientation lineage/delta rules into compact trigger-gated reference sections. A one-file skill can still have conditional sections, but the core must be visibly complete on its own and all escalations must state the evidence that loads them. Orientation remains append-only: the last complete snapshot is current; older snapshots remain immutable evidence and are never hot-path context.

**Acceptance evidence before calling v4 successful.** (1) Work v4 must be materially smaller than Intent plus Improve for ordinary decision-bearing loading, not merely smaller than all five suite files; (2) a genuine external non-software task must show outcome recovery, a rejected purpose-defeating route, mapping, comparison, challenge, and pre-action Trail evidence; (3) a Micro and Standard pair must demonstrate proportional context loading without historical Orientation or full-Trail default reads; (4) repeated Orient runs must preserve prior snapshots byte-for-byte while the latest snapshot alone guides ordinary work; and (5) independent models must test the sharpened Intent bar before any convergence claim.

**Orient trigger and prediction.** The current Orientation's highest-leverage todo was to run this standalone Intent plus Improve assessment; it is now complete and the assessment materially changes the target model. I will append a current snapshot that retains the behavioral-conversion boundary, records the measured daily-use cost failure, and makes the v4 redesign argument the active candidate. **Outcome.** The structural redesign case held. v3.17.0 retains the named Intent and Improve-grade operations: direct inspection confirms one each of steps 0-4, one optional harness section, mandatory intent recovery before route selection, mandatory risk-sized mapping and route comparison for decision-bearing work, and a pre-decision structural challenge. Work is smaller than the full five-skill 77,118-character suite, but that is not the ordinary daily-use comparison the operator identified. This falsifies the present economy claim without claiming that the preserved reasoning operations are absent.

**Redesign decision.** A v4 reconstruction is warranted; another local v3 compression patch is not. The proposed architecture keeps one installable skill but rebuilds it from an explicit decision-bearing core: tier dispatch, always-on Intent recovery and route-legitimacy rejection, Improve-grade map/model/lenses/routes/challenge, tiered contemporaneous Trail, and triggered Orient. Destination interviews, evidence-triggered hunches, harness bounds, cross-target probes, convergence, Trail templates, and Orientation lineage become concise trigger-gated reference sections. Orientation remains append-only: the last complete snapshot is current and older snapshots remain immutable evidence, never routine context. No prior snapshot was altered to suppress them.

**Reflection.** [!REALIZATION] A consolidation is not token-efficient merely because it is one file or smaller than the entire suite. Its relevant comparator is the skill combination actually loaded for ordinary work. The new measurement exposes a product failure that internal wording audits could not: v3's capability preservation may be real in contract, but its daily-use economy has become unearned. **Across-trail triggers.** Deeper self-audit explicitly requested: fired. Recurring finding-class: fired - self-targeted compression has repeatedly accumulated mechanism and then required a storage/retrieval or context correction. Contradicts prior `[!REALIZATION]`: yes, the prior near-floor claim is falsified for the daily-use baseline; it held only against the full five-skill load, not the common Intent plus Improve path. This diff found a second coherent, contract-restorable class: **anti-confabulation auditing** - operations that treat the trail itself as evidence to be distrusted. This is the suite's core defense against the algorithmic-gaslighting threat, and the rewrite classified it as restatement.

**Also found, smaller, same-decision:** (d) no 30-second digest resolution - v3/Trail required outcome+delta fields so a triaging reader need not parse whole entries; (e) no verbatim-quote preference for the operator's load-bearing words (paraphrase is where interpretation drift hides); (f) "there is no such thing as just conversation" - a conversation that produces a decision owes an entry; v4's "run" can be read as tool-work only.

**Considered and declined, with reasons:** Orient scope statements (v4's trigger-plus-prediction recording covers the falsifiability need); parent-scope traversal inside Orient (lives in First contact, destination basis flows from there); freshness guards, record.py, history/learning/sessions derivations, fidelity taxonomy, writer splitting (all deliberately cut per confirmed destination - no tooling, no derived files).

**Lenses (named).** Purpose: the cut class defends the destination's own threat model - highest relevance. Inconsistency: v4 demands honest writing but not distrustful reading; asymmetric. Overburden: none - additions are small. Waste: none found in the diffed sources beyond what the destination already cut.

**[!DECISION] One incremental change, with prediction.** Add compactly to v4: arc-audit duties in Orient's open pass (outcome anchoring, reversal-density suspicion, diff-vs-claim on suspicion); a one-line outcome digest closing Standard/Full entries; verbatim-quote preference for load-bearing operator words; the decision-in-conversation clause. Version 4.2.0. Prediction: file stays under 17,000 characters (under 0.59x the 28,886 baseline); edits touch only the version line, step 4's opening line and bullets, and Orient procedure step 1; no tier boundary, append-only rule, or escalation trigger changes; diagnostics and assertions pass. I expect a third coherent cut class NOT to surface from Intent/Destination diffs - those were the least procedural sources - but that is now a claim under test, not an assumption.

**[!REVERSAL] Mid-run collision with an interrupted prior turn, disclosed.** A previous turn ("ok lets do that") was cut off mid-execution and turned out to have HALF-applied its own version of these restorations before dying: a dedicated Orient step 2 ("Audit the trail before trusting it"), a standalone digest/quotes bullet, a README status update, and the 4.2.0 version bump were already in the working tree, unnoticed until this run's batch partially collided with them. Reconciliation: kept the interrupted turn's richer dedicated Orient audit step and reverted this run's duplicate open-pass tail; merged its digest/quotes bullet into the Standard/Full bullet (one location, not two); kept its README update, which accurately bounds the final size. Lesson recorded: after any interrupted turn, diff the working tree before re-deriving the same change - the collision was caught by assertion counting (digest and rationalization mentions expected once each), which is exactly the kind of check that made the duplication visible instead of silently shipped.

**Outcome versus prediction.** Held with the reconciliation noted above: 16,783 characters (0.581x baseline, under the 17,000 bound); all seven assertions pass; duplication eliminated (one digest mention, one rationalization mention); `git diff --check` clean. The edit surface was slightly wider than predicted (README, plus Orient gained a dedicated step rather than an extended step 1) - both differences came from adopting the interrupted turn's work rather than from scope creep, and both are disclosed rather than absorbed.

**Falsifiable claim about the target.** v4's rewrite bias cut exactly two coherent operation classes - arc-level evaluation-recording (restored in 4.1.0) and anti-confabulation auditing (restored in 4.2.0) - and the remaining Intent/Destination diffs will surface no third class, only wording-level differences. A future diff that finds another coherent class falsifies this and reopens the question of whether the rewrite should be re-derived under Improve governance from scratch.

**Blind spot.** Both restoration passes diffed contracts against contracts. Nobody has yet watched a fresh model execute v4.2.0 cold and checked whether the restored duties actually fire in behavior - the digest line, the trail-audit step, and the verbatim-quote preference are exactly the kind of clauses a busy run quietly skips.

**Expert-reader pushback.** "You are converging back toward the suite one class at a time - at 16,783 characters and climbing, when does restoration become reconstruction?" Answer on record: the boundary is the confirmed destination's cut list (no tooling, no derived files, no writer splitting, no scoring rubric) plus the 28,886 baseline as a hard ceiling; 0.58x with both known classes restored suggests the compression thesis survives, but the trajectory is now evidence either way.

**History-trigger check.** Recurring finding-class: FIRED - three restoration findings in a row is the pattern; the macro read (this entry's falsifiable claim) states where it should stop. Imminent silence: not fired. Contradicted [!REALIZATION]: FIRED - the 4.1.0 entry's one-class claim was falsified by this run, marked there as invited. Operator request: FIRED - this run executed the operator-confirmed ranked move 1.

**Candidate next moves, ranked.** 1. Blind head-to-head external task (v4.2.0 vs Intent plus Improve) - the decisive acceptance evidence, and the answer to the blind spot above. 2. Diff v4 against intent/destination skills to close the falsifiable claim cheaply. 3. Re-run the external rater on v4.2.0 - free directional telemetry on the anti-confabulation restoration.

**Digest: falsified the one-class claim; restored the anti-confabulation audit class (arc outcome-anchoring, reversal-density suspicion, diff-vs-claim, digest line, verbatim quotes, decision-in-conversation) as v4.2.0 at 16,783 chars (0.58x baseline); reconciled a half-applied interrupted turn instead of duplicating it.**

Cost: moderate - 12 tool operations, 3 files written, 2 external skills read end-to-end, 0 subagents.

**Audit the trail before trusting it (v4's own new step, exercised on itself for the first time).** Predictions across all three restoration entries held on the stated bounds (size ceilings met each time) - but the pattern itself is suspicious by v4's own new rule: three consecutive entries each narrate "found exactly one (then one more) capability gap, fixed it, size still fine." Diff-vs-claim check: the size numbers are independently reproducible (character counts, re-run in this session, match each entry's claims) and the restored text is visibly present - so the entries are not fabricated. But reversal density is thin (one `[!REVERSAL]`, and it was a process collision, not a reasoning reversal) for four consecutive "successful" entries in a self-audited rewrite. This is exactly the shape v4's audit step now warns about. Named as the outside-rubric concern below rather than smoothed over.

**Rating and challenge.** Rubric re-derived rather than mechanically copied: axes 1/2/3/5/7 held flat (no new evidence touches their specific bars); axis 4 rose modestly (the restored anti-confabulation duty is a direct Observable Autonomy reinforcement); axis 6 held at 8 despite passing every bound, because the size ratio moved from 0.51x to 0.58x - capability restoration is spending the compression margin, not improving it further; axis 8 rose from 7 to 8 on the one real independent data point available (external rating 7.5 to 8.5, correlated with restoration, still thin); a new axis 9 was added specifically to rate the self-audit-honesty risk this arc has been accumulating, at a deliberately moderate 6/10. ## 2026-07-28 - improve-governed-diff-against-orient-and-trail

**Tier: Full. Model: Claude Fable 5 (GitHub Copilot). Governing process: `improve/SKILL.md` v3.10.0, same as the previous experiment. Operator selected ranked candidate 1 ("ok lets do that").**

**Interpretation.** Operation-diff v4.1.0 against `orient/SKILL.md` v2.0.0 (15,254 chars) and `trail/SKILL.md` v1.19.0 (18,388 chars), both read end-to-end. Purpose-defeating route named and rejected: "restoring" operations the destination deliberately excludes - record.py, derived history/learning files, mandatory session/transcript files, fidelity taxonomy, writer splitting, mandated per-iteration commits - would satisfy "close the gap with the suite" literally while violating the confirmed no-tooling, no-installer mandate. Those stay out, and this entry names them as excluded rather than missed.

**Lenses.** *Purpose:* v4 keeps Orient's rubric/snapshot/rules/bounded-silence operations and Trail's two-stage/markers/reconstruction-test operations. *Inconsistency:* v4.1.0's step 4 promises "a later Orient audits how selections diverged from rankings" but v4's Orient procedure never instructs that audit - a dangling contract. *Waste:* README's Status still cites the v4.0.1 character count, stale since v4.1.0. *Overburden:* nothing actionable.

**[!REALIZATION] Second coherent cut class found: trail-integrity distrust.** This falsifies the previous entry's recorded claim that the compression damage was concentrated in one class - marked here, not rewritten there. Orient v2.0.0 dedicates a third of its arc-read to distrusting the record itself: outcome anchoring (did past predictions actually hold over subsequent runs), reversal-density plausibility (an unbroken all-green trail indicates post-hoc rationalization, not excellence), diff-versus-claim adversarial audit, and avoided-corners attention analysis. Trail v1.19.0 carries the same class (suspect-green-trail rule, literal-quotes-over-paraphrase, the three-resolutions digest line). v4 tells the agent to read markers and re-derive measurements but never to distrust the trail it reads. Given the operator's core threat model - post-hoc rationalization as algorithmic gaslighting - this is the least acceptable class to have cut. Pattern across both passes: the rewrite bias systematically cut operations that do not help the current run but protect the arc - first arc-level reflection, now arc-level distrust.

**[!DECISION] One incremental change, with prediction.** v4.2.0: (1) insert a trail-integrity audit operation into v4's Orient procedure (outcome anchoring, reversal-density plausibility, diff-versus-claim, avoided corners, ranking-versus-selection steering audit - resolving the dangling contract); (2) add to step 4 a one-line digest requirement (the 30-second resolution) and the literal-quotes-over-paraphrase rule; (3) refresh README's stale count. Prediction: file stays under 17,400 characters (below 0.61x baseline); Orient's operations renumber cleanly with no other behavioral change; no excluded-by-destination mechanism is reintroduced; diagnostics and `git diff --check` stay clean.
